File: collect.py | Lines: 172

------------------------------------------------------------
import os
import sys
import argparse
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Set, List, Iterator
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import filedialog

# --- Configuration Constants ---
DEFAULT_EXCLUDES = {
    "node_modules", ".git", "__pycache__", ".venv", "venv",
    ".next", "dist", "build", ".DS_Store"
}

# Added modern web and data formats
BINARY_EXTENSIONS = {
    ".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".gif", ".svg", ".ico", ".webp",
    ".mp4", ".mov", ".mp3", ".wav", ".exe", ".zip", ".pdf",
    ".db", ".sqlite", ".pyc", ".woff", ".woff2"
}


class ProjectCollector:
    def __init__(self, root: str, output: str, excludes: Set[str]):
        self.root = Path(root).resolve()
        self.output_path = Path(output).resolve()
        self.excludes = excludes
        self.total_lines = 0
        self.files_indexed = 0

    def is_binary(self, path: Path) -> bool:
        if path.suffix.lower() in BINARY_EXTENSIONS:
            return True

        try:
            with open(path, "rb") as f:
                return b"\x00" in f.read(8192)
        except OSError:
            return True

    def build_tree(self, curr_dir: Path, prefix: str = "") -> Iterator[str]:
        """Generator for a visual tree structure."""
        if curr_dir == self.root:
            yield f"{curr_dir.name}/"

        entries = sorted(
            [e for e in curr_dir.iterdir() if e.name not in self.excludes],
            key=lambda e: (not e.is_dir(), e.name.lower())
        )

        for i, entry in enumerate(entries):
            is_last = i == len(entries) - 1
            connector = "└── " if is_last else "├── "
            yield f"{prefix}{connector}{entry.name}{'/' if entry.is_dir() else ''}"

            if entry.is_dir():
                new_prefix = prefix + ("    " if is_last else "│   ")
                yield from self.build_tree(entry, new_prefix)

    def collect(self):
        text_files: List[Path] = []
        all_paths: List[Path] = []

        # Walk through files
        for path in self.root.rglob("*"):
            if any(part in self.excludes for part in path.parts):
                continue

            if path == self.output_path or path.is_dir():
                continue

            all_paths.append(path)

            if not self.is_binary(path):
                text_files.append(path)

        with open(self.output_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:

            # 1. Directory Tree
            out.write(f"{'=' * 60}\n  DIRECTORY TREE\n{'=' * 60}\n")
            out.write("\n".join(self.build_tree(self.root)) + "\n\n")

            # 2. File Index
            out.write(f"{'=' * 60}\n  FILE INDEX ({len(all_paths)} files)\n{'=' * 60}\n")

            for p in sorted(all_paths):
                out.write(f"{p.relative_to(self.root)}\n")

            out.write("\n")

            # 3. Contents
            out.write(f"{'=' * 60}\n  FILE CONTENTS\n{'=' * 60}\n")

            for path in text_files:
                try:
                    content = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
                    lines = content.count('\n') + 1
                    self.total_lines += lines

                    out.write(f"{'-' * 60}\n")
                    out.write(f"File: {path.name} | Lines: {lines}\n")
                    out.write(f"Path: {path.relative_to(self.root)}\n")
                    out.write(f"{'-' * 60}\n{content}\n\n")

                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"Error reading {path.name}: {e}")

        self.print_summary(len(all_paths), len(text_files))

    def print_summary(self, total, written):
        print(f"\n✨ Aggregation Complete!")
        print(f"📂 Output:  {self.output_path}")
        print(f"📊 Summary: {total} files found | {written} text files processed")
        print(f"📈 Total lines of code: {self.total_lines}")


def choose_folder():
    """Open a GUI folder picker."""
    root = tk.Tk()
    root.withdraw()  # Hide main tkinter window

    folder_selected = filedialog.askdirectory(
        title="Select Project Folder"
    )

    return folder_selected


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Collect project files into one text file."
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "-o",
        "--output",
        default="all_files.txt",
        help="Output filename"
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "-e",
        "--exclude",
        nargs="*",
        help="Additional directories to exclude"
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    # Ask user to choose folder using GUI
    selected_folder = choose_folder()

    if not selected_folder:
        print("❌ No folder selected.")
        sys.exit()

    excludes = DEFAULT_EXCLUDES.copy()

    if args.exclude:
        excludes.update(args.exclude)

    collector = ProjectCollector(
        selected_folder,
        args.output,
        excludes
    )

    collector.collect()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

------------------------------------------------------------
File: README_CHANGES.txt | Lines: 33
Path: README_CHANGES.txt
------------------------------------------------------------
AUTOMATIC TICKET ASSIGNMENT - what is in this zip
=================================================

Copy these folders over your project (keep your .env, uploads/, node_modules/
and package.json files):

  server/                      new layered backend (routes / controllers / services)
  Frontendapp/src/assets/component/AssignedTickets.jsx   queued badge + waiting message
  Frontendapp/src/assets/component/TicketDrawer.jsx      clearer note for staff

Delete the old server/index.js logic: the new server/index.js replaces it.

RULES
  1. A tech is available when he has no open / in_progress ticket on hand.
  2. Several free techs: fewest tickets handled first (even spread).
  3. Nobody free: ticket is queued on the first tech (lowest id).
  4. When any tech finishes, a waiting ticket moves to whoever is free first.
  High priority tickets are served before older low priority ones.

DATABASE
  Two columns are added to table "ticket" automatically at startup
  (queued, assigned_at). If your DB user cannot ALTER, run:
    ALTER TABLE ticket
      ADD COLUMN queued TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 AFTER assigned_to,
      ADD COLUMN assigned_at DATETIME NULL AFTER queued;

TESTS (from the server folder)
  node --test test/ticketAssignment.test.js test/dispatcher.test.js test/errorHandling.test.js

OPTIONAL .env
  PORT=3000
  DISPATCH_INTERVAL_MS=60000

File: README.md | Lines: 17

------------------------------------------------------------
# React + Vite

This template provides a minimal setup to get React working in Vite with HMR and some ESLint rules.

Currently, two official plugins are available:

- [@vitejs/plugin-react](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react) uses [Oxc](https://oxc.rs)
- [@vitejs/plugin-react-swc](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react-swc) uses [SWC](https://swc.rs/)

## React Compiler

The React Compiler is not enabled on this template because of its impact on dev & build performances. To add it, see [this documentation](https://react.dev/learn/react-compiler/installation).

## Expanding the ESLint configuration

If you are developing a production application, we recommend using TypeScript with type-aware lint rules enabled. Check out the [TS template](https://github.com/vitejs/vite/tree/main/packages/create-vite/template-react-ts) for information on how to integrate TypeScript and [`typescript-eslint`](https://typescript-eslint.io) in your project.

File: app.js | Lines: 16

import express from 'express'
import cors from 'cors'
import routes from './routes/index.js'
import errorHandler from './middleware/errorHandler.js'
import { UPLOAD_DIR } from './middleware/upload.js'

const app = express()

app.use(cors())
app.use(express.json())
app.use('/uploads', express.static(UPLOAD_DIR)) // ticket attachments
app.use(routes)
app.use(errorHandler)

export default app


------------------------------------------------------------
File: index.js | Lines: 28
Path: server\index.js
------------------------------------------------------------
import { config } from './config/env.js' // loads .env first
import { checkDatabase } from './config/db.js'
import app from './app.js'
import { verifyMailer } from './services/notification.service.js'
import { assigner } from './services/assigner.js'

checkDatabase()
verifyMailer()

// Prepare the database for auto-assignment, serve any waiting tickets, then
// re-check every minute as a safety net
assigner
  .ensureSchema()
  .then(() => {
    assigner.dispatch()
    assigner.startSweep(config.dispatchIntervalMs)
  })
  .catch((err) =>
    console.error(
      'Auto-assignment setup failed. Add the columns by hand: ' +
        'ALTER TABLE ticket ADD COLUMN queued TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 AFTER assigned_to, ' +
        'ADD COLUMN assigned_at DATETIME NULL AFTER queued;',
      err.message
    )
  )

app.listen(config.port, () => console.log(`Server is running on port ${config.port}`))

import { Routes, Route, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import Login from './assets/component/login'
import ProtectedRoute from './assets/component/protectedRoute'
import Layout from './assets/component/layout'
import StaffDashboard from './assets/component/StaffDashboard'
import Landing from './assets/component/landing'
import ITHome from './assets/component/ITHome'
import AssignedTickets from './assets/component/AssignedTickets'
import AvailableTickets from './assets/component/AvailableTickets'
import AdminHome from './assets/component/AdminHome'
import AllTickets from './assets/component/AllTickets'
import Users from './assets/component/Users'
import Categories from './assets/component/Categories'
import Statistics from './assets/component/Statistics'

const itLinks = [
  { to: '/it', label: 'Dashboard', end: true, icon: 'speedometer2' },
  { to: '/it/available', label: 'Available tickets', icon: 'inbox' },
  { to: '/it/tickets', label: 'Assigned tickets', icon: 'wrench-adjustable' },
]
const adminLinks = [
  { to: '/admin', label: 'Dashboard', end: true, icon: 'speedometer2' },
  { to: '/admin/tickets', label: 'All tickets', icon: 'ticket-detailed' },
  { to: '/admin/users', label: 'Users', icon: 'people' },
  { to: '/admin/categories', label: 'Categories', icon: 'tags' },
  { to: '/admin/statistics', label: 'Statistics', icon: 'bar-chart' },
]

function App() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route path="/" element={<Landing />} />
      <Route path="/login" element={<Login />} />

      {/* Staff: one page, no sidebar */}
      <Route
        path="/dashboard"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute role="staff">
            <StaffDashboard />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      />
      <Route path="/dashboard/*" element={<Navigate to="/dashboard" replace />} />

      <Route
        path="/it"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute role="it_user">
            <Layout title="IT support space" links={itLinks} />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      >
        <Route index element={<ITHome />} />
        <Route path="available" element={<AvailableTickets />} />
        <Route path="tickets" element={<AssignedTickets />} />
      </Route>

      <Route
        path="/admin"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute role="admin">
            <Layout title="Administration" links={adminLinks} />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      >
        <Route index element={<AdminHome />} />
        <Route path="tickets" element={<AllTickets />} />
        <Route path="users" element={<Users />} />
        <Route path="categories" element={<Categories />} />
        <Route path="statistics" element={<Statistics />} />
      </Route>
    </Routes>
  )
}

export default App

File: api.js | Lines: 26
Path: Frontendapp\src\services\api.js
------------------------------------------------------------
import axios from 'axios'

const api = axios.create({ baseURL: 'http://localhost:3000' })

// Send the login token with every request
api.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('token')
  if (token) config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`
  return config
})

// If the server says "not logged in" (token expired after 8h), go back to the login page
api.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  (error) => {
    const isLoginRequest = error.config?.url === '/login'
    if (error.response?.status === 401 && !isLoginRequest) {
      localStorage.clear()
      window.location.href = '/login'
    }
    return Promise.reject(error)
  }
)

export default api

File: Format.js | Lines: 11

// Small helpers to show dates in the user's own language and format
export const fmtDate = (d) =>
  d ? new Date(d).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' }) : ''

export const fmtDateTime = (d) =>
  d
    ? new Date(d).toLocaleString(undefined, {
        day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
      })
    : ''

File: AdminHome.jsx | Lines: 48
Path: Frontendapp\src\assets\component\AdminHome.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import api from '../../services/api'
import StatCard from './StatCard'

const AdminHome = () => {
  const [stats, setStats] = useState(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    api.get('/stats').then((res) => setStats(res.data)).catch(console.error)
  }, [])

  if (!stats) return <p>Loading...</p>

  return (
    <>
      <div className="row g-3 mb-4">
        <StatCard label="Users" value={stats.users} color="primary" />
        <StatCard label="All tickets" value={stats.tickets} color="dark" />
        <StatCard label="Open" value={stats.open} color="danger" />
        <StatCard label="In progress" value={stats.in_progress} color="warning" />
        <StatCard label="Resolved" value={stats.resolved} color="success" />
        <StatCard label="Closed" value={stats.closed} color="secondary" />
      </div>

      <div className="card">
        <div className="card-body">
          <h5 className="mb-3">Tickets by category</h5>
          {stats.byCategory.length === 0 && <p className="text-muted mb-0">No data yet</p>}
          {stats.byCategory.map((c) => (
            <div className="mb-3" key={c.name}>
              <div className="d-flex justify-content-between small">
                <span>{c.name}</span><span>{c.total}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="progress" style={{ height: 8 }}>
                <div
                  className="progress-bar bg-success"
                  style={{ width: `${stats.tickets ? (c.total / stats.tickets) * 100 : 0}%` }}
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

export default AdminHome

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import api from '../../services/api'
import AttachmentLink from './AttachmentLink'

const badge = {
  open: 'primary',
  in_progress: 'warning',
  resolved: 'success',
  closed: 'secondary',
}

const AllTickets = () => {
  const [tickets, setTickets] = useState([])
  const [techs, setTechs] = useState([])
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('all')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const load = () =>
    api.get('/tickets')
      .then((res) => setTickets(res.data))
      .catch(() => setError('Could not load tickets'))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false))

  useEffect(() => {
    load()
    api.get('/users', { params: { role: 'it_user' } })
      .then((res) => setTechs(res.data))
      .catch(console.error)
  }, [])

  const assign = async (id, userId) => {
    if (!userId) return
    setError('')
    try {
      await api.put(`/tickets/${id}/assign`, { assigned_to: userId })
      load()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Could not assign the ticket')
    }
  }

  const shown = filter === 'all' ? tickets : tickets.filter((t) => t.status === filter)

  if (loading) return <p>Loading...</p>

  return (
    <>
      {error && <div className="alert alert-danger py-2">{error}</div>}

      <div className="card">
        <div className="card-body table-responsive">
          <div className="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-3">
            <h5 className="mb-0">All tickets ({shown.length})</h5>
            <select
              className="form-select form-select-sm w-auto"
              value={filter}
              onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value)}
            >
              <option value="all">All</option>
              <option value="open">Open</option>
              <option value="in_progress">In progress</option>
              <option value="resolved">Resolved</option>
              <option value="closed">Closed</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <table className="table align-middle">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>#</th><th>Title</th><th>Created by</th><th>Category</th>
                <th>Priority</th><th>Status</th><th>Assigned to</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {shown.length === 0 && (
                <tr><td colSpan="7" className="text-center text-muted">No tickets</td></tr>
              )}
              {shown.map((t) => (
                <tr key={t.id}>
                  <td>{t.id}</td>
                  <td>
                    <div>{t.title}</div>
                    <AttachmentLink ticket={t} />
                  </td>
                  <td>{t.created_by_name}</td>
                  <td>{t.category_name}</td>
                  <td>{t.priority}</td>
                  <td>
                    <span className={`badge text-bg-${badge[t.status]}`}>
                      {t.status.replace('_', ' ')}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td style={{ minWidth: 180 }}>
                    <select
                      className="form-select form-select-sm"
                      value={t.assigned_to || ''}
                      disabled={t.status === 'closed'}
                      onChange={(e) => assign(t.id, e.target.value)}
                    >
                      <option value="">Not assigned</option>
                      {techs.map((u) => (
                        <option key={u.id} value={u.id}>{u.name}</option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

export default AllTickets

File: AssignedTickets.jsx | Lines: 194
Path: Frontendapp\src\assets\component\AssignedTickets.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import api from '../../services/api'
import AttachmentLink from './AttachmentLink'

const badge = {
  open: 'primary',
  in_progress: 'warning',
  resolved: 'success',
  closed: 'secondary',
}

const AssignedTickets = () => {
  const [tickets, setTickets] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('all')
  const [resolving, setResolving] = useState(null) // the ticket being resolved
  const [note, setNote] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const load = () =>
    api.get('/tickets/assigned')
      .then((res) => setTickets(res.data))
      .catch(() => setError('Could not load tickets'))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false))

  useEffect(() => { load() }, [])

  const takeCharge = async (id) => {
    setError('')
    try {
      await api.put(`/tickets/${id}/take-charge`)
      load()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Action failed')
    }
  }

  const updateStatus = async (id, status) => {
    setError('')
    try {
      await api.put(`/tickets/${id}/status`, { status })
      load()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Action failed')
    }
  }

  const submitResolution = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setError('')
    try {
      await api.put(`/tickets/${resolving.id}/resolve`, { resolution: note })
      setResolving(null)
      setNote('')
      load()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Could not resolve the ticket')
    }
  }

  const shown = filter === 'all' ? tickets : tickets.filter((t) => t.status === filter)

  if (loading) return <p>Loading...</p>

  return (
    <>
      {error && <div className="alert alert-danger py-2">{error}</div>}

      <div className="card">
        <div className="card-body table-responsive">
          <div className="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-3">
            <h5 className="mb-0">Assigned tickets</h5>
            <select
              className="form-select form-select-sm w-auto"
              value={filter}
              onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value)}
            >
              <option value="all">All</option>
              <option value="open">To start</option>
              <option value="in_progress">In progress</option>
              <option value="resolved">Resolved</option>
              <option value="closed">Closed</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <table className="table align-middle">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>#</th><th>Title</th><th>Created by</th>
                <th>Priority</th><th>Status</th><th>Action</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {shown.length === 0 && (
                <tr><td colSpan="6" className="text-center text-muted">No tickets</td></tr>
              )}
              {shown.map((t) => (
                <tr key={t.id}>
                  <td>{t.id}</td>
                  <td>
                    <div className="fw-semibold">{t.title}</div>
                    <div className="small text-muted">{t.description}</div>
                    <AttachmentLink ticket={t} />
                  </td>
                  <td>{t.created_by_name}</td>
                  <td>{t.priority}</td>
                  <td>
                    {t.queued ? (
                      <span className="badge text-bg-info">queued</span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className={`badge text-bg-${badge[t.status]}`}>
                        {t.status.replace('_', ' ')}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    {t.status === 'open' && t.queued === 1 && (
                      <span className="text-muted small">
                        Waiting: starts when a technician is free
                      </span>
                    )}
                    {t.status === 'open' && !t.queued && (
                      <button className="btn btn-sm btn-primary" onClick={() => takeCharge(t.id)}>
                        Start
                      </button>
                    )}
                    {t.status === 'in_progress' && (
                      <div className="d-flex gap-2">
                        <select
                          className="form-select form-select-sm"
                          value={t.status}
                          onChange={(e) => updateStatus(t.id, e.target.value)}
                        >
                          <option value="open">Open</option>
                          <option value="in_progress">In progress</option>
                        </select>
                        <button
                          className="btn btn-sm btn-success"
                          onClick={() => setResolving(t)}
                        >
                          Resolve
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {(t.status === 'resolved' || t.status === 'closed') && (
                      <span className="text-muted small">Done</span>
                    )}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Resolve form */}
      {resolving && (
        <div
          className="position-fixed top-0 start-0 w-100 h-100 d-flex justify-content-center align-items-center"
          style={{ background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)', zIndex: 1050 }}
        >
          <div className="card" style={{ width: 480 }}>
            <form className="card-body" onSubmit={submitResolution}>
              <h5>Resolve ticket #{resolving.id}</h5>
              <p className="text-muted small mb-3">{resolving.title}</p>
              <label className="form-label fw-semibold">What was done to fix it?</label>
              <textarea
                className="form-control mb-3"
                rows="4"
                value={note}
                onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Describe the solution"
                required
              />
              <div className="d-flex justify-content-end gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="btn btn-outline-secondary"
                  onClick={() => { setResolving(null); setNote('') }}
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button className="btn btn-success">Mark as resolved</button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

export default AssignedTickets

File: AttachmentLink.jsx | Lines: 19
Path: Frontendapp\src\assets\component\AttachmentLink.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------
const API_URL = 'http://localhost:3000'

// Small "paper clip" link to the file attached to a ticket (shows nothing if there is none)
const AttachmentLink = ({ ticket }) => {
  if (!ticket.attachment) return null
  return (
    <a
      href={`${API_URL}/uploads/${ticket.attachment}`}
      target="_blank"
      rel="noreferrer"
      className="small d-inline-block"
    >
      <i className="bi bi-paperclip"></i> {ticket.attachment_name || 'Attachment'}
    </a>
  )
}

export default AttachmentLink

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import api from '../../services/api'
import AttachmentLink from './AttachmentLink'

const AvailableTickets = () => {
  const [tickets, setTickets] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState('')

  const load = () =>
    api.get('/tickets/unassigned')
      .then((res) => setTickets(res.data))
      .catch(() => setError('Could not load tickets'))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false))

  useEffect(() => { load() }, [])

  const takeCharge = async (t) => {
    setError('')
    setSuccess('')
    try {
      await api.put(`/tickets/${t.id}/take-charge`)
      setSuccess(`Ticket #${t.id} is now yours. Find it in "Assigned tickets".`)
    } catch (err) {
      // 409 = someone else was faster
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Could not take this ticket')
    }
    load()
  }

  if (loading) return <p>Loading...</p>

  return (
    <>
      {error && <div className="alert alert-danger py-2">{error}</div>}
      {success && <div className="alert alert-success py-2">{success}</div>}

      <div className="card">
        <div className="card-body table-responsive">
          <div className="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-3">
            <h5 className="mb-0">Available tickets ({tickets.length})</h5>
            <button className="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary" onClick={load}>
              Refresh
            </button>
          </div>

          <table className="table align-middle">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>#</th><th>Title</th><th>Created by</th><th>Category</th>
                <th>Priority</th><th>Created</th><th></th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {tickets.length === 0 && (
                <tr><td colSpan="7" className="text-center text-muted">No tickets waiting</td></tr>
              )}
              {tickets.map((t) => (
                <tr key={t.id}>
                  <td>{t.id}</td>
                  <td>
                    <div className="fw-semibold">{t.title}</div>
                    <div className="small text-muted">{t.description}</div>
                    <AttachmentLink ticket={t} />
                  </td>
                  <td>{t.created_by_name}</td>
                  <td>{t.category_name}</td>
                  <td>{t.priority}</td>
                  <td>{new Date(t.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</td>
                  <td>
                    <button className="btn btn-sm btn-primary" onClick={() => takeCharge(t)}>
                      Take charge
                    </button>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

export default AvailableTickets

File: Categories.jsx | Lines: 115
Path: Frontendapp\src\assets\component\Categories.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import api from '../../services/api'

const Categories = () => {
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState([])
  const [name, setName] = useState('')
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState(null)
  const [editName, setEditName] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const load = () =>
    api.get('/categories').then((res) => setCategories(res.data)).catch(console.error)

  useEffect(() => { load() }, [])

  const add = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setError('')
    try {
      await api.post('/categories', { name })
      setName('')
      load()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Could not add the category')
    }
  }

  const saveEdit = async (id) => {
    setError('')
    try {
      await api.put(`/categories/${id}`, { name: editName })
      setEditingId(null)
      load()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Could not rename the category')
    }
  }

  const remove = async (c) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Delete the category "${c.name}"?`)) return
    setError('')
    try {
      await api.delete(`/categories/${c.id}`)
      load()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Could not delete (it may be used by tickets)')
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="card" style={{ maxWidth: 650 }}>
      <div className="card-body">
        <h5 className="mb-3">Categories</h5>
        {error && <div className="alert alert-danger py-2">{error}</div>}

        <form onSubmit={add} className="d-flex gap-2 mb-4">
          <input
            className="form-control"
            placeholder="New category name (e.g. Network)"
            value={name}
            onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
            required
          />
          <button className="btn btn-success">Add</button>
        </form>

        <ul className="list-group">
          {categories.length === 0 && (
            <li className="list-group-item text-muted">No categories yet</li>
          )}
          {categories.map((c) => (
            <li key={c.id}
              className="list-group-item d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center gap-2">
              {editingId === c.id ? (
                <>
                  <input
                    className="form-control form-control-sm"
                    value={editName}
                    onChange={(e) => setEditName(e.target.value)}
                  />
                  <div className="d-flex gap-2">
                    <button className="btn btn-sm btn-success" onClick={() => saveEdit(c.id)}>
                      Save
                    </button>
                    <button className="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary"
                      onClick={() => setEditingId(null)}>
                      Cancel
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <span>{c.name}</span>
                  <div className="d-flex gap-2">
                    <button
                      className="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary"
                      onClick={() => { setEditingId(c.id); setEditName(c.name) }}
                    >
                      Rename
                    </button>
                    <button className="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger" onClick={() => remove(c)}>
                      Delete
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </>
              )}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export default Categories

------------------------------------------------------------
File: CreateTicket.jsx | Lines: 86
Path: Frontendapp\src\assets\component\CreateTicket.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import api from '../../services/api'

const CreateTicket = () => {
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState([])
  const [values, setValues] = useState({
    title: '', description: '', category_id: '', priority: 'medium',
  })
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const navigate = useNavigate()

  useEffect(() => {
    api.get('/categories').then((res) => setCategories(res.data)).catch(console.error)
  }, [])

  const handleChange = (e) =>
    setValues({ ...values, [e.target.name]: e.target.value })

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setError('')
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      await api.post('/tickets', values)
      navigate('/dashboard/tickets')
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Could not create the ticket')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="card" style={{ maxWidth: 650 }}>
      <div className="card-body">
        <h5 className="mb-3">Create a ticket</h5>
        {error && <div className="alert alert-danger py-2">{error}</div>}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <div className="mb-3">
            <label className="form-label fw-semibold">Title</label>
            <input name="title" value={values.title} onChange={handleChange}
              className="form-control" placeholder="Short summary of the problem" required />
          </div>

          <div className="mb-3">
            <label className="form-label fw-semibold">Description</label>
            <textarea name="description" rows="4" value={values.description}
              onChange={handleChange} className="form-control"
              placeholder="Explain what happened" required />
          </div>

          <div className="row">
            <div className="col-md-6 mb-3">
              <label className="form-label fw-semibold">Category</label>
              <select name="category_id" value={values.category_id}
                onChange={handleChange} className="form-select" required>
                <option value="">Choose...</option>
                {categories.map((c) => (
                  <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.name}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
            <div className="col-md-6 mb-3">
              <label className="form-label fw-semibold">Priority</label>
              <select name="priority" value={values.priority}
                onChange={handleChange} className="form-select">
                <option value="low">Low</option>
                <option value="medium">Medium</option>
                <option value="high">High</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button className="btn btn-success" disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? 'Sending...' : 'Submit ticket'}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export default CreateTicket

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import api from '../../services/api'
import StatCard from './StatCard'

const badge = {
  open: 'primary',
  in_progress: 'warning',
  resolved: 'success',
  closed: 'secondary',
}

const ITHome = () => {
  const [tickets, setTickets] = useState([])

  useEffect(() => {
    api.get('/tickets/assigned').then((res) => setTickets(res.data)).catch(console.error)
  }, [])

  const count = (s) => tickets.filter((t) => t.status === s).length

  return (
    <>
      <div className="row g-3 mb-4">
        <StatCard label="Assigned to me" value={tickets.length} color="primary" />
        <StatCard label="To start" value={count('open')} color="danger" />
        <StatCard label="In progress" value={count('in_progress')} color="warning" />
        <StatCard label="Resolved" value={count('resolved')} color="success" />
      </div>

      <div className="card">
        <div className="card-body table-responsive">
          <h5 className="mb-3">Latest assigned tickets</h5>
          <table className="table align-middle mb-0">
            <thead>
              <tr><th>#</th><th>Title</th><th>Priority</th><th>Status</th></tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {tickets.length === 0 && (
                <tr><td colSpan="4" className="text-center text-muted">Nothing assigned yet</td></tr>
              )}
              {tickets.slice(0, 5).map((t) => (
                <tr key={t.id}>
                  <td>{t.id}</td>
                  <td>{t.title}</td>
                  <td>{t.priority}</td>
                  <td>
                    <span className={`badge text-bg-${badge[t.status]}`}>
                      {t.status.replace('_', ' ')}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

export default ITHome

import { useState } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import './landing.css'

const navLinks = [
  { label: 'Home', href: '#home' },
  { label: 'Features', href: '#features' },
  { label: 'About', href: '#about' },
  { label: 'Contact', href: '#contact' },
]

const features = [
  {
    title: 'Report an Incident',
    text: 'Employees can easily report hardware and software problems with priority tagging and categories.',
    icon: 'ticket-detailed-fill',
  },
  {
    title: 'IT Support Routing',
    text: 'Technicians take charge of tickets, log resolutions, and manage workflows in real-time.',
    icon: 'headset',
  },
  {
    title: 'Live Notifications',
    text: 'Stay informed at every step with automatic notification alerts and resolution status tracking.',
    icon: 'bell-fill',
  },
]

const Landing = () => {
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false)

        <nav className={`landing-nav ${menuOpen ? 'open' : ''}`}>

File: layout.jsx | Lines: 78

import { useState } from 'react'
import { NavLink, Outlet, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import NotificationBell from './NotificationBell'
import './dashboard.css'

const roleNames = { staff: 'Staff', it_user: 'IT support', admin: 'Administrator' }

const Layout = ({ title, links }) => {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const roleLabel = roleNames[localStorage.getItem('role')] || ''
  const name = localStorage.getItem('name') || roleLabel

  const logout = () => {
    localStorage.clear()
    navigate('/login')
  }

  return (
    <div className="app-shell">
      {open && <div className="sidebar-backdrop" onClick={() => setOpen(false)} />}

      <aside className={'sidebar' + (open ? ' open' : '')}>
        <div className="brand">
          <i className="bi bi-headset"></i> ANOV
        </div>

        <nav>
          {links.map((l) => (
            <NavLink
              key={l.to}
              to={l.to}
              end={l.end}
              onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
              className={({ isActive }) => 'side-link' + (isActive ? ' active' : '')}
            >
              <i className={`bi bi-${l.icon || 'circle'}`}></i> {l.label}
            </NavLink>
          ))}
        </nav>

        <button className="side-link mt-auto border-0 bg-transparent text-start" onClick={logout}>
          <i className="bi bi-box-arrow-left"></i> Logout
        </button>
      </aside>

      <div className="main">
        <header className="topbar">
          <div className="d-flex align-items-center gap-2" style={{ minWidth: 0 }}>
            <button
              className="btn btn-light d-lg-none"
              onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
              aria-label="Open menu"
            >
              <i className="bi bi-list fs-5"></i>
            </button>
            <h5 className="mb-0 fw-semibold text-truncate">{title}</h5>
          </div>

          <div className="d-flex align-items-center gap-3">
            <NotificationBell />
            <div className="text-end lh-sm d-none d-sm-block">
              <div className="fw-semibold small">{name}</div>
              <div className="text-muted" style={{ fontSize: '.75rem' }}>{roleLabel}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="avatar">{(name || '?').charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</div>
          </div>
        </header>

        <main className="content">
          <Outlet />
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export default Layout

------------------------------------------------------------
File: login.jsx | Lines: 125
Path: Frontendapp\src\assets\component\login.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import api from '../../services/api'
import './style.css'

const Login = () => {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({ email: '', password: '' })
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const navigate = useNavigate()

  const handleChange = (e) =>
    setValues({ ...values, [e.target.name]: e.target.value })

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (values.password.length < 6) {
      setError('Password must be at least 6 characters')
      return
    }
    setError('')
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      const { data } = await api.post('/login', values)
      localStorage.setItem('token', data.token)
      localStorage.setItem('role', data.role)
      localStorage.setItem('name', data.name)

      if (data.role === 'admin') navigate('/admin')
      else if (data.role === 'it_user') navigate('/it')
      else navigate('/dashboard')
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Cannot reach the server')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="loginPage">
      <div className="loginform">
        <div className="login-brand-icon">
          <i className="bi bi-headset"></i>
        </div>

        <h2>Welcome Back</h2>
        <p className="subtitle">Sign in to your portal</p>

        {error && (
          <div className="alert alert-danger py-2 d-flex align-items-center gap-2 small" role="alert">
            <i className="bi bi-exclamation-triangle-fill flex-shrink-0"></i>
            <div>{error}</div>
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <div className="mb-3">
            <label htmlFor="email" className="form-label">
              <i className="bi bi-envelope me-1 text-muted"></i> Email address
            </label>
            <input
              type="email"
              id="email"
              name="email"
              value={values.email}
              onChange={handleChange}
              autoComplete="email"
              placeholder="name@globalservice.com"
              className="form-control"
              required
            />
          </div>

          <div className="mb-4">
            <label htmlFor="password" className="form-label">
              <i className="bi bi-lock me-1 text-muted"></i> Password
            </label>
            <div className="input-group">
              <input
                type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                id="password"
                name="password"
                value={values.password}
                onChange={handleChange}
                autoComplete="current-password"
                placeholder="Enter your password"
                className="form-control"
                required
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn btn-outline-secondary"
                onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                aria-label={showPassword ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password'}
              >
                <i className={`bi bi-${showPassword ? 'eye-slash' : 'eye'}`}></i>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button type="submit" className="btn btn-submit" disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? (
              <>
                <span className="spinner-border spinner-border-sm me-2" role="status" aria-hidden="true"></span>
                Signing In...
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                Sign In <i className="bi bi-arrow-right-short fs-5"></i>
              </>
            )}
          </button>

          <Link to="/" className="login-back-link">
            <i className="bi bi-arrow-left me-1"></i> Back to Homepage
          </Link>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export default Login

File: NotificationBell.jsx | Lines: 114
Path: Frontendapp\src\assets\component\NotificationBell.jsx

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import api from '../../services/api'

const NotificationBell = () => {
  const [items, setItems] = useState([])
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const box = useRef(null)

  const load = useCallback(() => {
    api.get('/notifications')
      .then((res) => setItems(res.data))
      .catch(() => {}) // the bell should never break the page
  }, [])

  // Load now, then check for new notifications every 30 seconds
  useEffect(() => {
    load()
    const timer = setInterval(load, 30000)
    return () => clearInterval(timer)
  }, [load])

  // Close the list when the user clicks somewhere else
  useEffect(() => {
    const onClick = (e) => {
      if (box.current && !box.current.contains(e.target)) setOpen(false)
    }
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', onClick)
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', onClick)
  }, [])

  const unread = items.filter((n) => !n.is_read).length

  const markRead = async (n) => {
    if (n.is_read) return
    setItems((list) => list.map((x) => (x.id === n.id ? { ...x, is_read: 1 } : x)))
    try {
      await api.put(`/notifications/${n.id}/read`)
    } catch {
      load() // put the real state back if the server refused
    }
  }

  const markAllRead = async () => {
    setItems((list) => list.map((x) => ({ ...x, is_read: 1 })))
    try {
      await api.put('/notifications/read-all')
    } catch {
      load()
    }
  }

  const toggle = () => {
    if (!open) load() // refresh when opening
    setOpen(!open)
  }

  return (
    <div className="position-relative" ref={box}>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="btn btn-light position-relative"
        onClick={toggle}
        aria-label="Notifications"
      >
        <i className="bi bi-bell fs-5"></i>
        {unread > 0 && (
          <span className="position-absolute top-0 start-100 translate-middle badge rounded-pill bg-danger">
            {unread > 99 ? '99+' : unread}
          </span>
        )}
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div
          className="position-absolute end-0 mt-2 bg-white border rounded shadow"
          style={{ width: 'min(340px, 70vw)', maxHeight: 420, overflowY: 'auto', zIndex: 1050 }}
        >
          <div className="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center px-3 py-2 border-bottom">
            <span className="fw-semibold">Notifications</span>
            {unread > 0 && (
              <button type="button" className="btn btn-link btn-sm p-0" onClick={markAllRead}>
                Mark all as read
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {items.length === 0 && (
            <div className="px-3 py-4 text-center text-muted small">No notifications yet</div>
          )}

          {items.map((n) => (
            <button
              type="button"
              key={n.id}
              onClick={() => markRead(n)}
              className={
                'd-block w-100 text-start border-0 border-bottom px-3 py-2 ' +
                (n.is_read ? 'bg-white text-muted' : 'bg-light fw-semibold')
              }
            >
              <div className="small">{n.message}</div>
              <div className="text-muted fw-normal" style={{ fontSize: '.72rem' }}>
                {new Date(n.created_at).toLocaleString()}
              </div>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export default NotificationBell


------------------------------------------------------------
File: protectedRoute.jsx | Lines: 20
Path: Frontendapp\src\assets\component\protectedRoute.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------
import { Navigate } from 'react-router-dom'

// Where each role lands after login
const homeOf = { staff: '/dashboard', it_user: '/it', admin: '/admin' }

const ProtectedRoute = ({ role, children }) => {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('token')
  const userRole = localStorage.getItem('role')

  // Not logged in -> login page
  if (!token || !userRole) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />

  // Logged in but on a page of another role -> send to their own space
  if (userRole !== role) return <Navigate to={homeOf[userRole] || '/login'} replace />

  return children
}

export default ProtectedRoute

------------------------------------------------------------
File: StaffDashboard.jsx | Lines: 332
Path: Frontendapp\src\assets\component\StaffDashboard.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import api from '../../services/api'
import { fmtDate } from '../../services/format'
import NotificationBell from './NotificationBell'
import StatusPill from './StatusPill'
import TicketDrawer from './TicketDrawer'
import './dashboard.css'
import './staff.css'

const emptyForm = { title: '', description: '', category_id: '', priority: 'medium' }
const MAX_FILE_MB = 5

const StaffDashboard = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const name = localStorage.getItem('name') || 'Staff'

  const [tickets, setTickets] = useState([])
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [message, setMessage] = useState({ type: '', text: '' })

  // ticket details panel
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState(null)
  const [closing, setClosing] = useState(false)
  const [drawerError, setDrawerError] = useState('')
  const selected = tickets.find((t) => t.id === selectedId) || null

  // "New ticket" modal
  const [showModal, setShowModal] = useState(false)
  const [values, setValues] = useState(emptyForm)
  const [file, setFile] = useState(null)
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState('')
  const [sending, setSending] = useState(false)

  const load = () =>
    api.get('/tickets/my')
      .then((res) => setTickets(res.data))
      .catch(() => setMessage({ type: 'danger', text: 'Could not load your tickets' }))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false))

  useEffect(() => {
    load()
    api.get('/categories').then((res) => setCategories(res.data)).catch(console.error)
  }, [])

  // Escape closes whatever is open on top
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!showModal && !selectedId) return
    const onKey = (e) => {
      if (e.key !== 'Escape') return
      if (showModal) setShowModal(false)
      else setSelectedId(null)
    }
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey)
  }, [showModal, selectedId])

  const logout = () => {
    localStorage.clear()
    navigate('/login')
  }

  const openDetails = (id) => {
    setDrawerError('')
    setSelectedId(id)
  }

  const openModal = () => {
    setValues(emptyForm)
    setFile(null)
    setFormError('')
    setShowModal(true)
  }

  const handleChange = (e) => setValues({ ...values, [e.target.name]: e.target.value })

  const handleFile = (e) => {
    const chosen = e.target.files[0] || null
    if (chosen && chosen.size > MAX_FILE_MB * 1024 * 1024) {
      setFormError(`File is too large (max ${MAX_FILE_MB} MB)`)
      e.target.value = ''
      setFile(null)
      return
    }
    setFormError('')
    setFile(chosen)
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setFormError('')
    setSending(true)
    try {
      // FormData is needed to send a file together with the text fields
      const data = new FormData()
      data.append('title', values.title)
      data.append('description', values.description)
      data.append('category_id', values.category_id)
      data.append('priority', values.priority)
      if (file) data.append('attachment', file)

      await api.post('/tickets', data)
      setShowModal(false)
      setMessage({ type: 'success', text: 'Your ticket was sent to the IT team.' })
      load()
    } catch (err) {
      setFormError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Could not create the ticket')
    } finally {
      setSending(false)
    }
  }

  const closeTicket = async (t) => {
    setDrawerError('')
    setClosing(true)
    try {
      await api.put(`/tickets/${t.id}/close`)
      setSelectedId(null)
      setMessage({ type: 'success', text: `Ticket #${t.id} is closed.` })
      load()
    } catch (err) {
      setDrawerError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Could not close the ticket')
    } finally {
      setClosing(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="app-shell staff-page">
      <div className="main">
        <header className="topbar">
          <div className="sp-brand">
            <i className="bi bi-headset"></i> ANOV
          </div>

          <div className="d-flex align-items-center gap-3">
            <NotificationBell />
            <div className="text-end lh-sm d-none d-sm-block">
              <div className="fw-semibold small">{name}</div>
              <div className="sp-muted" style={{ fontSize: '.75rem' }}>Staff</div>
            </div>
            <div className="avatar">{name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</div>
            <button type="button" className="sp-btn sp-btn-quiet sp-btn-small" onClick={logout}>
              Log out
            </button>
          </div>
        </header>

        <main className="content">
          <div className="sp-wrap">
            <div className="sp-head">
              <div>
                <h1 className="sp-title">My tickets</h1>
                <p className="sp-sub">Everything you have sent to the IT team, and where it stands.</p>
              </div>
              <button type="button" className="sp-btn sp-btn-primary" onClick={openModal}>
                <i className="bi bi-plus-lg"></i> New ticket
              </button>
            </div>

            {message.text && (
              <div className={`alert alert-${message.type} py-2`}>{message.text}</div>
            )}

            <div className="sp-table-wrap">
              {loading ? (
                <p className="sp-empty">Loading your tickets...</p>
              ) : tickets.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="sp-empty">
                  <strong>No tickets yet</strong>
                  <p>When something is not working, send it to the IT team.</p>
                  <button type="button" className="sp-btn sp-btn-primary" onClick={openModal}>
                    <i className="bi bi-plus-lg"></i> New ticket
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <table className="sp-table">
                  <thead>
                    <tr>
                      <th className="sp-hide-sm">No.</th>
                      <th>Ticket</th>
                      <th className="sp-hide-sm">Priority</th>
                      <th>Status</th>
                      <th className="sp-hide-sm">Handled by</th>
                      <th className="sp-hide-sm">Created</th>
                      <th></th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {tickets.map((t) => (
                      <tr
                        key={t.id}
                        className="sp-row"
                        tabIndex={0}
                        onClick={() => openDetails(t.id)}
                        onKeyDown={(e) => {
                          if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
                            e.preventDefault()
                            openDetails(t.id)
                          }
                        }}
                      >
                        <td className="sp-id sp-hide-sm">#{t.id}</td>
                        <td className="sp-ticket-cell">
                          <div className="sp-ticket-title">
                            {t.title}
                            {t.attachment && (
                              <i className="bi bi-paperclip sp-clip" title="Has an attachment"></i>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <div className="sp-ticket-meta">{t.category_name || 'No category'}</div>
                        </td>
                        <td className={'sp-cap sp-hide-sm ' + (t.priority === 'high' ? 'sp-high' : 'sp-muted')}>
                          {t.priority}
                        </td>
                        <td><StatusPill status={t.status} /></td>
                        <td className="sp-hide-sm sp-nowrap">
                          {t.assigned_to_name || <span className="sp-muted">Not assigned yet</span>}
                        </td>
                        <td className="sp-hide-sm sp-nowrap sp-muted">{fmtDate(t.created_at)}</td>
                        <td className="sp-chevron"><i className="bi bi-chevron-right"></i></td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>

      {/* Ticket details */}
      {selected && (
        <TicketDrawer
          ticket={selected}
          error={drawerError}
          closing={closing}
          onClose={() => setSelectedId(null)}
          onCloseTicket={closeTicket}
        />
      )}

      {/* New ticket */}
      {showModal && (
        <div
          className="sp-overlay sp-center"
          onMouseDown={(e) => { if (e.target === e.currentTarget) setShowModal(false) }}
        >
          <form className="sp-dialog" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            <div className="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
              <h2 className="sp-dialog-title">New ticket</h2>
              <button
                type="button" className="sp-x" aria-label="Close"
                onClick={() => setShowModal(false)}
              >
                <i className="bi bi-x-lg"></i>
              </button>
            </div>

            {formError && <div className="alert alert-danger py-2">{formError}</div>}

            <div className="mb-3">
              <label className="form-label">Title</label>
              <input
                name="title" value={values.title} onChange={handleChange}
                className="form-control" placeholder="Short summary of the problem"
                required autoFocus
              />
            </div>

            <div className="mb-3">
              <label className="form-label">Description</label>
              <textarea
                name="description" rows="4" value={values.description} onChange={handleChange}
                className="form-control" placeholder="Explain what happened" required
              />
            </div>

            <div className="row">
              <div className="col-sm-6 mb-3">
                <label className="form-label">Category</label>
                <select
                  name="category_id" value={values.category_id} onChange={handleChange}
                  className="form-select" required
                >
                  <option value="">Choose...</option>
                  {categories.map((c) => (
                    <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.name}</option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div className="col-sm-6 mb-3">
                <label className="form-label">Priority</label>
                <select
                  name="priority" value={values.priority} onChange={handleChange}
                  className="form-select"
                >
                  <option value="low">Low</option>
                  <option value="medium">Medium</option>
                  <option value="high">High</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mb-4">
              <label className="form-label">
                Attach a file <span className="sp-muted fw-normal">(optional, max {MAX_FILE_MB} MB)</span>
              </label>
              <input
                type="file" className="form-control" onChange={handleFile}
                accept=".png,.jpg,.jpeg,.gif,.pdf,.doc,.docx,.xls,.xlsx,.txt"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="d-flex justify-content-end gap-2">
              <button type="button" className="sp-btn sp-btn-quiet" onClick={() => setShowModal(false)}>
                Cancel
              </button>
              <button className="sp-btn sp-btn-primary" disabled={sending}>
                {sending ? 'Sending...' : 'Submit ticket'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export default StaffDashboard

File: Statistics.jsx | Lines: 71
Path: Frontendapp\src\assets\component\Statistics.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import api from '../../services/api'
import StatCard from './StatCard'

const BarList = ({ title, rows, total, color }) => (
  <div className="card h-100">
    <div className="card-body">
      <h5 className="mb-3">{title}</h5>
      {rows.length === 0 && <p className="text-muted mb-0">No data yet</p>}
      {rows.map((r) => (
        <div className="mb-3" key={r.label}>
          <div className="d-flex justify-content-between small">
            <span>{r.label}</span><span>{r.total}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="progress" style={{ height: 8 }}>
            <div
              className={`progress-bar bg-${color}`}
              style={{ width: `${total ? (r.total / total) * 100 : 0}%` }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  </div>
)

const Statistics = () => {
  const [stats, setStats] = useState(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    api.get('/stats').then((res) => setStats(res.data)).catch(console.error)
  }, [])

  if (!stats) return <p>Loading...</p>

  const byStatus = [
    { label: 'Open', total: stats.open },
    { label: 'In progress', total: stats.in_progress },
    { label: 'Resolved', total: stats.resolved },
    { label: 'Closed', total: stats.closed },
  ]
  const byPriority = (stats.byPriority || []).map((p) => ({ label: p.priority, total: p.total }))
  const byCategory = stats.byCategory.map((c) => ({ label: c.name, total: c.total }))
  const done = stats.resolved + stats.closed
  const rate = stats.tickets ? Math.round((done / stats.tickets) * 100) : 0

  return (
    <>
      <div className="row g-3 mb-4">
        <StatCard label="Total tickets" value={stats.tickets} color="dark" />
        <StatCard label="Resolved or closed" value={done} color="success" />
        <StatCard label="Resolution rate" value={`${rate}%`} color="primary" />
        <StatCard label="Still open" value={stats.open + stats.in_progress} color="danger" />
      </div>

      <div className="row g-3">
        <div className="col-lg-4">
          <BarList title="By status" rows={byStatus} total={stats.tickets} color="primary" />
        </div>
        <div className="col-lg-4">
          <BarList title="By priority" rows={byPriority} total={stats.tickets} color="warning" />
        </div>
        <div className="col-lg-4">
          <BarList title="By category" rows={byCategory} total={stats.tickets} color="success" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

export default Statistics

File: TicketDrawer.jsx | Lines: 93

import AttachmentLink from './AttachmentLink'
import StatusPill from './StatusPill'
import { fmtDateTime } from '../../services/format'

// Side panel with every detail of one ticket
const TicketDrawer = ({ ticket: t, error, closing, onClose, onCloseTicket }) => {
  const notes = {
    open: t.assigned_to_name
      ? t.queued
        ? `All technicians are busy. ${t.assigned_to_name} has your ticket in the queue and it will go to the first technician who is free.`
        : `${t.assigned_to_name} has been assigned and will start soon.`
      : 'Waiting for the IT team to pick this up.',
    in_progress: t.assigned_to_name
      ? `${t.assigned_to_name} is working on this.`
      : 'The IT team is working on this.',
    resolved: 'The IT team marked this as resolved. Close the ticket if the problem is fixed.',
    closed: 'This ticket is closed.',
  }

  return (
    <div
      className="sp-overlay"
      onMouseDown={(e) => { if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose() }}
    >
      <aside className="sp-drawer" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={`Ticket ${t.id}`}>
        <div className="sp-drawer-top">
          <span>Ticket #{t.id}</span>
          <button
            type="button" className="sp-x" aria-label="Close details"
            onClick={onClose} autoFocus
          >
            <i className="bi bi-x-lg"></i>
          </button>
        </div>

        <h2>{t.title}</h2>
        <StatusPill status={t.status} />

        <dl className="sp-facts">
          <dt>Category</dt>
          <dd>{t.category_name || 'None'}</dd>
          <dt>Priority</dt>
          <dd className="sp-cap">{t.priority}</dd>
          <dt>Handled by</dt>
          <dd>{t.assigned_to_name || 'Not assigned yet'}</dd>
          <dt>Created</dt>
          <dd>{fmtDateTime(t.created_at)}</dd>
          {t.closed_at && (
            <>
              <dt>Closed</dt>
              <dd>{fmtDateTime(t.closed_at)}</dd>
            </>
          )}
        </dl>

        <h3 className="sp-section">Description</h3>
        <p className="sp-text">{t.description}</p>

        {t.resolution && (
          <>
            <h3 className="sp-section">Solution from IT</h3>
            <p className="sp-text sp-solution">{t.resolution}</p>
          </>
        )}

        {t.attachment && (
          <>
            <h3 className="sp-section">Attachment</h3>
            <div className="sp-text">
              <AttachmentLink ticket={t} />
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        <div className="sp-drawer-foot">
          {error && <div className="alert alert-danger py-2">{error}</div>}
          <p className="sp-note">{notes[t.status]}</p>
          {t.status === 'resolved' && (
            <button
              type="button" className="sp-btn sp-btn-primary"
              onClick={() => onCloseTicket(t)} disabled={closing}
            >
              {closing ? 'Closing...' : 'Close ticket'}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </aside>
    </div>
  )
}

export default TicketDrawer

File: Users.jsx | Lines: 132
Path: Frontendapp\src\assets\component\Users.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import api from '../../services/api'

const roleLabel = { staff: 'Staff', it_user: 'IT user', admin: 'Admin' }
const roleColor = { staff: 'primary', it_user: 'warning', admin: 'dark' }

const Users = () => {
  const [users, setUsers] = useState([])
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ name: '', email: '', password: '', role: 'staff' })
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  const load = () =>
    api.get('/users').then((res) => setUsers(res.data)).catch(console.error)

  useEffect(() => { load() }, [])

  const handleChange = (e) => setForm({ ...form, [e.target.name]: e.target.value })

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setError('')
    setSuccess('')
    if (form.password.length < 6) {
      setError('Password must be at least 6 characters')
      return
    }
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      await api.post('/users', form)
      setForm({ name: '', email: '', password: '', role: 'staff' })
      setSuccess('Account created')
      load()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Could not create the account')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  const remove = async (u) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Delete the account of ${u.name}?`)) return
    setError('')
    setSuccess('')
    try {
      await api.delete(`/users/${u.id}`)
      load()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Could not delete the account')
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <div className="card mb-4">
        <div className="card-body">
          <h5 className="mb-3">Create an account</h5>
          {error && <div className="alert alert-danger py-2">{error}</div>}
          {success && <div className="alert alert-success py-2">{success}</div>}

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="row g-3">
            <div className="col-md-6">
              <label className="form-label fw-semibold">Name</label>
              <input name="name" value={form.name} onChange={handleChange}
                className="form-control" required />
            </div>
            <div className="col-md-6">
              <label className="form-label fw-semibold">Email</label>
              <input type="email" name="email" value={form.email} onChange={handleChange}
                className="form-control" required />
            </div>
            <div className="col-md-6">
              <label className="form-label fw-semibold">Password</label>
              <input type="password" name="password" value={form.password}
                onChange={handleChange} className="form-control" required />
            </div>
            <div className="col-md-6">
              <label className="form-label fw-semibold">Role</label>
              <select name="role" value={form.role} onChange={handleChange}
                className="form-select">
                <option value="staff">Staff</option>
                <option value="it_user">IT user</option>
                <option value="admin">Admin</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div className="col-12">
              <button className="btn btn-success" disabled={loading}>
                {loading ? 'Creating...' : 'Create account'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="card">
        <div className="card-body table-responsive">
          <h5 className="mb-3">All users ({users.length})</h5>
          <table className="table align-middle">
            <thead>
              <tr><th>#</th><th>Name</th><th>Email</th><th>Role</th><th></th></tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {users.length === 0 && (
                <tr><td colSpan="5" className="text-center text-muted">No users</td></tr>
              )}
              {users.map((u) => (
                <tr key={u.id}>
                  <td>{u.id}</td>
                  <td>{u.name}</td>
                  <td>{u.email}</td>
                  <td>
                    <span className={`badge text-bg-${roleColor[u.role]}`}>
                      {roleLabel[u.role]}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="text-end">
                    <button className="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger" onClick={() => remove(u)}>
                      Delete
                    </button>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

export default Users

import fs from 'fs'
import asyncHandler from '../middleware/asyncHandler.js'
import * as ticketService from '../services/ticket.service.js'

/* ---------- STAFF ---------- */

export const create = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const ticket = await ticketService.createTicket(req.user.id, req.body, req.file)
    res.status(201).json(ticket)
  } catch (err) {
    // the ticket was not created: remove the file we just saved
    if (req.file) fs.unlink(req.file.path, () => {})
    throw err
  }
})

export const mine = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  res.json(await ticketService.listMyTickets(req.user.id))
})

export const close = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  await ticketService.closeTicket(req.params.id, req.user.id)
  res.json({ ok: true })
})

/* ---------- IT USER ---------- */

export const unassigned = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  res.json(await ticketService.listUnassigned())
})

export const assigned = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  res.json(await ticketService.listAssigned(req.user.id))
})

export const takeCharge = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  await ticketService.takeCharge(req.params.id, req.user.id)
  res.json({ ok: true })
})

export const updateStatus = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  await ticketService.updateStatus(req.params.id, req.user.id, req.body.status)
  res.json({ ok: true })
})

export const resolve = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  await ticketService.resolveTicket(req.params.id, req.user.id, req.body.resolution)
  res.json({ ok: true })
})

/* ---------- ADMIN ---------- */

export const listAll = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  res.json(await ticketService.listAllTickets())
})

export const assign = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  await ticketService.assignTicket(req.params.id, req.body.assigned_to)
  res.json({ ok: true })
})

  res.json(await userService.listUsers(req.query.role))
})

export const create = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const user = await userService.createUser(req.body)
  res.status(201).json(user)
})

export const remove = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  await userService.deleteUser(req.params.id, req.user.id)
  res.json({ ok: true })
})

// Sends errors from route handlers (async or not) to the error middleware,
// so the server never crashes
const asyncHandler = (fn) => async (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    await fn(req, res, next)
  } catch (err) {
    next(err)
  }
}

export default asyncHandler


------------------------------------------------------------
File: auth.js | Lines: 22
Path: server\middleware\auth.js
------------------------------------------------------------
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken'
import { config } from '../config/env.js'

// Checks the login token and puts the user in req.user
export function auth(req, res, next) {
  const header = req.headers.authorization || ''
  const token = header.startsWith('Bearer ') ? header.slice(7) : null
  if (!token) return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Not logged in' })
  try {
    req.user = jwt.verify(token, config.jwtSecret)
    next()
  } catch {
    res.status(401).json({ error: 'Session expired, please log in again' })
  }
}

// Allows only some roles on a route
export const only = (...roles) => (req, res, next) =>
  roles.includes(req.user.role)
    ? next()
    : res.status(403).json({ error: 'You are not allowed to do this' })

File: errorHandler.js | Lines: 12

import HttpError from '../utils/HttpError.js'

// Last middleware: one place that turns every error into a JSON answer
// eslint-disable-next-line no-unused-vars
export default function errorHandler(err, req, res, next) {
  if (err instanceof HttpError) {
    return res.status(err.status).json({ error: err.message })
  }
  console.error(err)
  res.status(500).json({ error: 'Server error' })
}

File: upload.js | Lines: 38
Path: server\middleware\upload.js

import multer from 'multer'
import path from 'path'
import fs from 'fs'
import { fileURLToPath } from 'url'

// Files are saved in the "uploads" folder at the root of the server
const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url))
export const UPLOAD_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'uploads')
fs.mkdirSync(UPLOAD_DIR, { recursive: true })

const ALLOWED_FILES = ['.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.gif', '.pdf', '.doc', '.docx', '.xls', '.xlsx', '.txt']

const upload = multer({
  storage: multer.diskStorage({
    destination: UPLOAD_DIR,
    // random name so two files with the same name never overwrite each other
    filename: (req, file, cb) => {
      const ext = path.extname(file.originalname).toLowerCase()
      cb(null, `${Date.now()}-${Math.round(Math.random() * 1e9)}${ext}`)
    },
  }),
  limits: { fileSize: 5 * 1024 * 1024 }, // 5 MB
  fileFilter: (req, file, cb) => {
    const ext = path.extname(file.originalname).toLowerCase()
    if (ALLOWED_FILES.includes(ext)) return cb(null, true)
    cb(new Error('Allowed files: images, PDF, Word, Excel or text'))
  },
})

// Reads one optional file sent in the field "attachment"
export function uploadAttachment(req, res, next) {
  upload.single('attachment')(req, res, (err) => {
    if (!err) return next()
    const msg = err.code === 'LIMIT_FILE_SIZE' ? 'File is too large (max 5 MB)' : err.message
    res.status(400).json({ error: msg })
  })
}

File: auth.service.js | Lines: 24
Path: server\services\auth.service.js
------------------------------------------------------------
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs'
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken'
import { db } from '../config/db.js'
import { config } from '../config/env.js'
import HttpError from '../utils/HttpError.js'

export async function login(email, password) {
  if (!email || !password) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Email and password are required')
  }

  const [rows] = await db.query('SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?', [email])
  const user = rows[0]
  if (!user) throw new HttpError(401, 'Invalid email or password')

  const ok = await bcrypt.compare(password, user.password)
  if (!ok) throw new HttpError(401, 'Invalid email or password')

  const token = jwt.sign({ id: user.id, role: user.role }, config.jwtSecret, {
    expiresIn: config.jwtExpiresIn,
  })
  return { token, role: user.role, name: user.name }
}

const cleanName = (name) => {
  const clean = (name || '').trim()
  if (!clean) throw new HttpError(400, 'Name is required')
  return clean

  const [rows] = await db.query('SELECT id, name FROM category ORDER BY name')
  return rows

export async function createCategory(name) {
  const clean = cleanName(name)
  const [r] = await db.query('INSERT INTO category (name) VALUES (?)', [clean])
  return { id: r.insertId, name: clean }
}
