  const clean = cleanName(name)
  await db.query('UPDATE category SET name = ? WHERE id = ?', [clean, id])
}

export async function deleteCategory(id) {
  try {
    await db.query('DELETE FROM category WHERE id = ?', [id])
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.code === 'ER_ROW_IS_REFERENCED_2') {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'This category is used by tickets and cannot be deleted')
    }
    throw err
  }
}

File: notification.service.js | Lines: 69
Path: server\services\notification.service.js
------------------------------------------------------------
import nodemailer from 'nodemailer'
import { db } from '../config/db.js'
import { config } from '../config/env.js'

// Email is optional: it only works if MAIL_USER and MAIL_PASS are in .env
const mailer =
  config.mail.user && config.mail.pass
    ? nodemailer.createTransport({
        service: 'gmail',
        auth: { user: config.mail.user, pass: config.mail.pass },
      })
    : null

// Called once at startup: tells if email is working
export function verifyMailer() {
  if (!mailer) {
    console.log('Email is disabled. Check MAIL_USER and MAIL_PASS.')
    return Promise.resolve()
  }
  return mailer
    .verify()
    .then(() => console.log('Gmail connection successful!'))
    .catch((err) => console.error('Gmail connection failed:', err.message))
}

// Creates the bell notification AND sends the email. Never throws.
export async function notify(userId, ticketId, message) {
  try {
    await db.query(
      'INSERT INTO notification (user_id, ticket_id, message) VALUES (?, ?, ?)',
      [userId, ticketId, message]
    )
    if (mailer) {
      const [rows] = await db.query('SELECT email FROM users WHERE id = ?', [userId])
      if (rows[0]) {
        mailer
          .sendMail({
            from: `"GLOBAL SERVICE" <${config.mail.user}>`,
            to: rows[0].email,
            subject: 'Ticket update',
            text: message,
          })
          .catch((err) => console.error('Email failed:', err.message))
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Notification failed:', err.message)
  }
}

export async function listForUser(userId) {
  const [rows] = await db.query(
    'SELECT * FROM notification WHERE user_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 30',
    [userId]
  )
  return rows
}

export async function markAllRead(userId) {
  await db.query('UPDATE notification SET is_read = 1 WHERE user_id = ?', [userId])
}

export async function markRead(notificationId, userId) {
  await db.query(
    'UPDATE notification SET is_read = 1 WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?',
    [notificationId, userId]
  )
}

File: stats.service.js | Lines: 35
Path: server\services\stats.service.js

import { db } from '../config/db.js'

export async function getStats() {
  const [[u]] = await db.query('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM users')
  const [[s]] = await db.query(`
    SELECT COUNT(*) AS tickets,
           COALESCE(SUM(status = 'open'), 0) AS \`open\`,
           COALESCE(SUM(status = 'in_progress'), 0) AS in_progress,
           COALESCE(SUM(status = 'resolved'), 0) AS resolved,
           COALESCE(SUM(status = 'closed'), 0) AS closed
    FROM ticket
  `)
  const [byCategory] = await db.query(`
    SELECT c.name, COUNT(t.id) AS total
    FROM category c
    LEFT JOIN ticket t ON t.category_id = c.id
    GROUP BY c.id, c.name
    ORDER BY total DESC
  `)
  const [byPriority] = await db.query(
    'SELECT priority, COUNT(*) AS total FROM ticket GROUP BY priority'
  )

  return {
    users: Number(u.n),
    tickets: Number(s.tickets),
    open: Number(s.open),
    in_progress: Number(s.in_progress),
    resolved: Number(s.resolved),
    closed: Number(s.closed),
    byCategory: byCategory.map((c) => ({ name: c.name, total: Number(c.total) })),
    byPriority: byPriority.map((p) => ({ priority: p.priority, total: Number(p.total) })),
  }
}


------------------------------------------------------------
File: ticket.service.js | Lines: 194
Path: server\services\ticket.service.js
------------------------------------------------------------
import { db } from '../config/db.js'
import { PRIORITIES } from '../config/constants.js'
import HttpError from '../utils/HttpError.js'
import { notify } from './notification.service.js'
import { assigner } from './assigner.js'

// Ticket list query with the names filled in
const TICKET_SELECT = `
  SELECT t.*,
         c.name AS category_name,
         u.name AS created_by_name,
         a.name AS assigned_to_name
  FROM ticket t
  LEFT JOIN category c ON c.id = t.category_id
  LEFT JOIN users u ON u.id = t.created_by
  LEFT JOIN users a ON a.id = t.assigned_to
`

// Small helper: one ticket's title + owner, used to build notifications
async function getTicketInfo(ticketId, columns) {
  const [[t]] = await db.query(`SELECT ${columns} FROM ticket WHERE id = ?`, [ticketId])
  return t
}

/* ---------- STAFF ---------- */

export async function createTicket(userId, { title, description, category_id, priority }, file) {
  if (!title?.trim() || !description?.trim()) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Title and description are required')
  }
  const cleanTitle = title.trim()

  const [r] = await db.query(
    `INSERT INTO ticket (title, description, category_id, priority, created_by, attachment, attachment_name)
     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
    [
      cleanTitle,
      description.trim(),
      category_id || null,
      PRIORITIES.includes(priority) ? priority : 'medium',
      userId,
      file ? file.filename : null,
      file ? file.originalname : null,
    ]
  )
  const id = r.insertId

  await notify(userId, id, `Your ticket "${cleanTitle}" was created`)

  // Automatic assignment: free tech with the lowest workload, or the queue
  await assigner.dispatch()

  return { id }
}

export async function listMyTickets(userId) {
  const [rows] = await db.query(
    `${TICKET_SELECT} WHERE t.created_by = ? ORDER BY t.created_at DESC`,
    [userId]
  )
  return rows
}

// Close one of my resolved tickets
export async function closeTicket(ticketId, userId) {
  const [r] = await db.query(
    `UPDATE ticket SET status = 'closed', closed_at = NOW()
     WHERE id = ? AND created_by = ? AND status = 'resolved'`,
    [ticketId, userId]
  )
  if (r.affectedRows === 0) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Only your resolved tickets can be closed')
  }
  const t = await getTicketInfo(ticketId, 'title, assigned_to')
  if (t.assigned_to) {
    await notify(t.assigned_to, ticketId, `Ticket "${t.title}" was closed by the user`)
  }
}

/* ---------- IT USER ---------- */

// Tickets nobody has taken yet
export async function listUnassigned() {
  const [rows] = await db.query(
    `${TICKET_SELECT} WHERE t.assigned_to IS NULL AND t.status = 'open'
     ORDER BY t.created_at DESC`
  )
  return rows
}

// Tickets assigned to one tech
export async function listAssigned(techId) {
  const [rows] = await db.query(
    `${TICKET_SELECT} WHERE t.assigned_to = ? ORDER BY t.created_at DESC`,
    [techId]
  )
  return rows
}

// Take charge of a ticket (a queued ticket cannot be started yet)
export async function takeCharge(ticketId, techId) {
  const [r] = await db.query(
    `UPDATE ticket SET assigned_to = ?, status = 'in_progress'
     WHERE id = ? AND status = 'open' AND queued = 0
       AND (assigned_to IS NULL OR assigned_to = ?)`,
    [techId, ticketId, techId]
  )
  if (r.affectedRows === 0) {
    const [[q]] = await db.query(
      'SELECT queued FROM ticket WHERE id = ? AND assigned_to = ?',
      [ticketId, techId]
    )
    throw new HttpError(
      409,
      q?.queued
        ? 'This ticket is in the queue. It will start as soon as a technician is free'
        : 'This ticket was already taken'
    )
  }
  const t = await getTicketInfo(ticketId, 'title, created_by')
  await notify(t.created_by, ticketId, `Your ticket "${t.title}" is now being handled`)
}

// Update the status of my ticket
export async function updateStatus(ticketId, techId, status) {
  if (!['open', 'in_progress'].includes(status)) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid status')
  }
  const [r] = await db.query(
    `UPDATE ticket SET status = ?
     WHERE id = ? AND assigned_to = ? AND status IN ('open', 'in_progress')`,
    [status, ticketId, techId]
  )
  if (r.affectedRows === 0) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'You can only update your own active tickets')
  }
  const t = await getTicketInfo(ticketId, 'title, created_by')
  await notify(
    t.created_by,
    ticketId,
    `Your ticket "${t.title}" is now ${status.replace('_', ' ')}`
  )
}

// Resolve my ticket
export async function resolveTicket(ticketId, techId, resolutionText) {
  const resolution = (resolutionText || '').trim()
  if (!resolution) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Please describe how it was solved')
  }
  const [r] = await db.query(
    `UPDATE ticket SET status = 'resolved', resolution = ?
     WHERE id = ? AND assigned_to = ? AND status IN ('open', 'in_progress')`,
    [resolution, ticketId, techId]
  )
  if (r.affectedRows === 0) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'You can only resolve your own active tickets')
  }
  const t = await getTicketInfo(ticketId, 'title, created_by')
  await notify(t.created_by, ticketId, `Your ticket "${t.title}" has been resolved`)

  // This tech is free now: give him (or another free tech) the next waiting ticket
  await assigner.dispatch()
}

/* ---------- ADMIN ---------- */

export async function listAllTickets() {
  const [rows] = await db.query(`${TICKET_SELECT} ORDER BY t.created_at DESC`)
  return rows
}

// Manual assignment by an admin (it is a real assignment, never a queued one)
export async function assignTicket(ticketId, techId) {
  const [techs] = await db.query(
    "SELECT id FROM users WHERE id = ? AND role = 'it_user'",
    [techId]
  )
  if (!techs[0]) throw new HttpError(400, 'Choose a valid IT user')

  const [r] = await db.query(
    "UPDATE ticket SET assigned_to = ?, queued = 0, assigned_at = NOW() WHERE id = ? AND status <> 'closed'",
    [techs[0].id, ticketId]
  )
  if (r.affectedRows === 0) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Ticket not found or already closed')
  }
  const t = await getTicketInfo(ticketId, 'title')
  await notify(techs[0].id, ticketId, `Ticket "${t.title}" was assigned to you`)

  // The previous holder of this ticket may be free now
  await assigner.dispatch()
}


------------------------------------------------------------
File: ticketAssignment.service.js | Lines: 240
Path: server\services\ticketAssignment.service.js
------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Automatic ticket assignment
 * ===========================
 *
 * RULES
 *  1. A tech user is AVAILABLE when he has no work on hand, meaning no ticket
 *     that is 'open' or 'in_progress' and not waiting in the queue.
 *  2. Several techs are available -> the ticket goes to the one who has handled
 *     the fewest tickets so far, so the work is spread evenly. Ties are broken
 *     by "who waited longest since his last assignment", then by lowest id.
 *  3. Nobody is available -> the ticket is QUEUED on the FIRST tech
 *     (lowest id). It is a waiting ticket: it does not make him busy and he
 *     cannot start it yet.
 *  4. Every time a tech finishes (or anything else changes), the queue is
 *     dispatched again. A queued ticket moves to whichever tech is free first,
 *     even if it is not the first tech.
 *
 * Pending tickets are served by priority (high -> low), then oldest first.
 *
 * DATABASE (added automatically at startup by ensureSchema, or run by hand):
 *   ALTER TABLE ticket
 *     ADD COLUMN queued TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 AFTER assigned_to,
 *     ADD COLUMN assigned_at DATETIME NULL AFTER queued;
 */

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* 1. PURE DECISION LOGIC (no database, easy to unit test)             */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

const PRIORITY_RANK = { high: 0, medium: 1, low: 2 }

// Order pending tickets: highest priority first, then oldest, then lowest id
export function sortPending(pending) {
  return [...pending].sort(
    (a, b) =>
      (PRIORITY_RANK[a.priority] ?? 1) - (PRIORITY_RANK[b.priority] ?? 1) ||
      new Date(a.created_at) - new Date(b.created_at) ||
      a.id - b.id
  )
}

// "Fairest" tech first: fewest tickets handled, waited longest, lowest id
function byFairness(a, b) {
  return (
    a.total - b.total ||
    (a.lastAssignedAt ?? -Infinity) - (b.lastAssignedAt ?? -Infinity) ||
    a.id - b.id
  )
}

/**
 * Decide what to do with every pending ticket.
 *
 * @param techs   [{ id, name, active, total, lastAssignedAt }]
 *                active = tickets on his hand, total = tickets handled so far,
 *                lastAssignedAt = timestamp in ms or null
 * @param pending [{ id, priority, created_at, assigned_to }]
 *                assigned_to is null for a fresh ticket, or the tech who holds
 *                it in the queue
 * @returns a list of changes: { ticketId, techId, techName, queued, previousTechId }
 *          A ticket that stays where it is has no entry.
 */
export function planAssignments(techs, pending, now = Date.now()) {
  const pool = techs
    .map((t) => ({ ...t }))
    .sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id) // pool[0] is the "first tech"
  const plan = []
  let tick = 0

  for (const ticket of sortPending(pending)) {
    const free = pool.filter((t) => t.active === 0).sort(byFairness)

    if (free.length > 0) {
      // Rules 1 + 2 (and 4 when the ticket was waiting in the queue)
      const tech = free[0]
      tech.active += 1
      tech.total += 1
      tech.lastAssignedAt = now + tick++
      plan.push({
        ticketId: ticket.id,
        techId: tech.id,
        techName: tech.name,
        queued: false,
        previousTechId: ticket.assigned_to ?? null,
      })
    } else if (ticket.assigned_to == null && pool.length > 0) {
      // Rule 3: everybody is busy -> wait in the first tech's queue
      const first = pool[0]
      plan.push({
        ticketId: ticket.id,
        techId: first.id,
        techName: first.name,
        queued: true,
        previousTechId: null,
      })
    }
    // else: already queued and nobody is free yet -> leave it alone
  }
  return plan
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* 2. DATABASE LAYER                                                   */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

export function createAssigner({ db, notify, log = console }) {
  // Adds the two columns if they are missing (safe to run on every start)
  async function ensureSchema() {
    const [cols] = await db.query(
      `SELECT COLUMN_NAME FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS
       WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE() AND TABLE_NAME = 'ticket'
         AND COLUMN_NAME IN ('queued', 'assigned_at')`
    )
    const have = new Set(cols.map((c) => c.COLUMN_NAME))
    if (!have.has('queued')) {
      await db.query(
        'ALTER TABLE ticket ADD COLUMN queued TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 AFTER assigned_to'
      )
    }
    if (!have.has('assigned_at')) {
      await db.query('ALTER TABLE ticket ADD COLUMN assigned_at DATETIME NULL AFTER queued')
      // old tickets: use their creation date as the assignment date
      await db.query(
        'UPDATE ticket SET assigned_at = created_at WHERE assigned_to IS NOT NULL AND assigned_at IS NULL'
      )
    }
  }

  // One run of the dispatcher. Only one run can work at a time because it
  // locks the tech rows, so two tickets can never pick the same "free" tech.
  async function runOnce() {
    const conn = await db.getConnection()
    const applied = []
    try {
      await conn.beginTransaction()
      await conn.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE role = 'it_user' ORDER BY id FOR UPDATE")

      // Workload of every tech. Queued tickets are NOT counted: they are waiting.
      const [techRows] = await conn.query(
        `SELECT u.id, u.name,
                COALESCE(SUM(t.status IN ('open', 'in_progress')), 0) AS active,
                COUNT(t.id) AS total,
                MAX(t.assigned_at) AS last_assigned_at
         FROM users u
         LEFT JOIN ticket t ON t.assigned_to = u.id AND t.queued = 0
         WHERE u.role = 'it_user'
         GROUP BY u.id, u.name
         ORDER BY u.id`
      )
      const techs = techRows.map((r) => ({
        id: r.id,
        name: r.name,
        active: Number(r.active),
        total: Number(r.total),
        lastAssignedAt: r.last_assigned_at ? new Date(r.last_assigned_at).getTime() : null,
      }))

      // Tickets waiting for a tech: brand new ones and the queued ones
      const [pending] = await conn.query(
        `SELECT id, title, priority, created_at, created_by, assigned_to
         FROM ticket
         WHERE status = 'open' AND (queued = 1 OR assigned_to IS NULL)`
      )

      const plan = planAssignments(techs, pending)
      const byId = new Map(pending.map((t) => [t.id, t]))

      for (const step of plan) {
        // The extra WHERE keeps us from overriding an admin who just assigned it by hand
        const [r] = await conn.query(
          `UPDATE ticket
           SET assigned_to = ?, queued = ?, assigned_at = NOW()
           WHERE id = ? AND status = 'open' AND (queued = 1 OR assigned_to IS NULL)`,
          [step.techId, step.queued ? 1 : 0, step.ticketId]
        )
        if (r.affectedRows > 0) applied.push({ ...step, ticket: byId.get(step.ticketId) })
      }

      await conn.commit()
    } catch (err) {
      await conn.rollback().catch(() => {})
      throw err
    } finally {
      conn.release()
    }

    // Notifications go out only after the changes are saved
    for (const s of applied) {
      const title = s.ticket.title
      if (s.queued) {
        await notify(s.techId, s.ticketId, `Ticket "${title}" is in your queue. It will be moved to the first technician who is free`)
        await notify(s.ticket.created_by, s.ticketId, `All technicians are busy. Your ticket "${title}" is in the queue`)
      } else {
        await notify(s.techId, s.ticketId, `Ticket "${title}" was assigned to you`)
        await notify(s.ticket.created_by, s.ticketId, `Your ticket "${title}" was assigned to ${s.techName}`)
        if (s.previousTechId && s.previousTechId !== s.techId) {
          await notify(s.previousTechId, s.ticketId, `Ticket "${title}" was moved to ${s.techName} who was free first`)
        }
      }
    }
    return applied
  }

  // Runs never overlap inside this process. If a trigger arrives while a run
  // is busy, exactly one more run is done afterwards.
  let running = null
  let again = false
  async function dispatch() {
    if (running) {
      again = true
      return running
    }
    running = (async () => {
      try {
        do {
          again = false
          await runOnce()
        } while (again)
      } finally {
        running = null
      }
    })()
    return running
  }

  // Never throws: a failed dispatch must not break the request that triggered
  // it. The ticket stays pending and is picked up by the next run.
  const safeDispatch = () =>
    dispatch().catch((err) => log.error('Ticket dispatch failed:', err.message))

  // Safety net: catches anything a trigger missed (for example a crash)
  function startSweep(everyMs = 60000) {
    const timer = setInterval(safeDispatch, everyMs)
    timer.unref?.()
    return timer
  }

  return { ensureSchema, dispatch: safeDispatch, startSweep }
}

File: user.service.js | Lines: 61
Path: server\services\user.service.js
------------------------------------------------------------
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs'
import { db } from '../config/db.js'
import { ROLES } from '../config/constants.js'
import HttpError from '../utils/HttpError.js'
import { assigner } from './assigner.js'

export async function listUsers(role) {
  const [rows] = ROLES.includes(role)
    ? await db.query(
        'SELECT id, name, email, role, created_at FROM users WHERE role = ? ORDER BY id',
        [role]
      )
    : await db.query('SELECT id, name, email, role, created_at FROM users ORDER BY id')
  return rows
}

export async function createUser({ name, email, password, role }) {
  if (!name?.trim() || !email?.trim() || !password) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Name, email and password are required')
  }
  if (password.length < 6) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Password must be at least 6 characters')
  }
  if (!ROLES.includes(role)) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid role')
  }

  let id
  try {
    const hash = await bcrypt.hash(password, 10)
    const [r] = await db.query(
      'INSERT INTO users (name, email, password, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
      [name.trim(), email.trim(), hash, role]
    )
    id = r.insertId
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.code === 'ER_DUP_ENTRY') throw new HttpError(400, 'This email is already used')
    throw err
  }

  // A new tech is free: he can take waiting tickets right away
  if (role === 'it_user') await assigner.dispatch()
  return { id }
}

export async function deleteUser(id, currentUserId) {
  if (Number(id) === currentUserId) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'You cannot delete your own account')
  }
  try {
    await db.query('DELETE FROM users WHERE id = ?', [id])
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.code === 'ER_ROW_IS_REFERENCED_2') {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'This user has tickets and cannot be deleted')
    }
    throw err
  }
  // The "first tech" may have changed
  await assigner.dispatch()
}

File: dispatcher.test.js | Lines: 112
Path: server\test\dispatcher.test.js

import test from 'node:test'
import assert from 'node:assert/strict'
import { createAssigner } from '../services/ticketAssignment.service.js'

// A fake MySQL pool that answers the dispatcher's queries with prepared rows
function fakeDb({ techRows, pending, failOnUpdate = false }) {
  const log = []
  const conn = {
    beginTransaction: async () => log.push('BEGIN'),
    commit: async () => log.push('COMMIT'),
    rollback: async () => log.push('ROLLBACK'),
    release: () => log.push('RELEASE'),
    query: async (sql, params) => {
      if (sql.includes('FOR UPDATE')) { log.push('LOCK'); return [[]] }
      if (sql.includes('FROM users u')) return [techRows]
      if (sql.includes('FROM ticket') && sql.includes('queued = 1')) return [pending]
      if (sql.startsWith('UPDATE ticket')) {
        if (failOnUpdate) throw new Error('boom')
        log.push(`UPDATE ticket ${params[2]} -> tech ${params[0]} queued=${params[1]}`)
        return [{ affectedRows: 1 }]
      }
      throw new Error('unexpected query: ' + sql)
    },
  }
  return { log, getConnection: async () => conn }
}

const silent = { error: () => {} }
const rows = (...r) => r.map(([id, name, active, total]) => ({
  id, name, active, total, last_assigned_at: null,
}))

test('free tech gets the ticket inside a locked transaction, then notifications are sent', async () => {
  const db = fakeDb({
    techRows: rows([1, 'Ann', 1, 4], [2, 'Bob', 0, 2]),
    pending: [{ id: 7, title: 'VPN down', priority: 'high', created_at: '2026-01-01', created_by: 99, assigned_to: null }],
  })
  const sent = []
  const a = createAssigner({ db, notify: async (...args) => sent.push(args), log: silent })
  await a.dispatch()

  assert.deepEqual(db.log, ['BEGIN', 'LOCK', 'UPDATE ticket 7 -> tech 2 queued=0', 'COMMIT', 'RELEASE'])
  assert.deepEqual(sent.map((s) => s[0]), [2, 99]) // the tech, then the creator
  assert.match(sent[0][2], /assigned to you/)
})

test('everybody busy: ticket is queued on the first tech and both sides are told', async () => {
  const db = fakeDb({
    techRows: rows([1, 'Ann', 1, 4], [2, 'Bob', 2, 2]),
    pending: [{ id: 8, title: 'Printer', priority: 'low', created_at: '2026-01-01', created_by: 50, assigned_to: null }],
  })
  const sent = []
  const a = createAssigner({ db, notify: async (...args) => sent.push(args), log: silent })
  await a.dispatch()

  assert.ok(db.log.includes('UPDATE ticket 8 -> tech 1 queued=1'))
  assert.match(sent[0][2], /in your queue/)
  assert.match(sent[1][2], /All technicians are busy/)
})

test('moving a queued ticket also tells the tech who held it', async () => {
  const db = fakeDb({
    techRows: rows([1, 'Ann', 1, 4], [2, 'Bob', 0, 2]),
    pending: [{ id: 9, title: 'Wifi', priority: 'medium', created_at: '2026-01-01', created_by: 50, assigned_to: 1 }],
  })
  const sent = []
  const a = createAssigner({ db, notify: async (...args) => sent.push(args), log: silent })
  await a.dispatch()

  assert.deepEqual(sent.map((s) => s[0]), [2, 50, 1])
  assert.match(sent[2][2], /moved to Bob/)
})

test('a database error rolls back, sends nothing and never throws to the caller', async () => {
  const db = fakeDb({
    techRows: rows([1, 'Ann', 0, 0]),
    pending: [{ id: 1, title: 'x', priority: 'medium', created_at: '2026-01-01', created_by: 5, assigned_to: null }],
    failOnUpdate: true,
  })
  const sent = []
  const a = createAssigner({ db, notify: async (...args) => sent.push(args), log: silent })
  await assert.doesNotReject(a.dispatch())

  assert.ok(db.log.includes('ROLLBACK'))
  assert.ok(!db.log.includes('COMMIT'))
  assert.ok(db.log.includes('RELEASE'))
  assert.equal(sent.length, 0)
})

test('triggers that arrive during a run never overlap: one extra run follows', async () => {
  let concurrent = 0
  let maxConcurrent = 0
  let runs = 0
  const db = fakeDb({ techRows: [], pending: [] })
  const original = db.getConnection
  db.getConnection = async () => {
    runs++
    concurrent++
    maxConcurrent = Math.max(maxConcurrent, concurrent)
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 20))
    const c = await original()
    const release = c.release
    c.release = () => { concurrent--; release() }
    return c
  }
  const a = createAssigner({ db, notify: async () => {}, log: silent })
  await Promise.all([a.dispatch(), a.dispatch(), a.dispatch(), a.dispatch()])

  assert.equal(maxConcurrent, 1)
  assert.equal(runs, 2) // the first run + a single follow-up for all the late triggers
})

File: errorHandling.test.js | Lines: 52
Path: server\test\errorHandling.test.js
------------------------------------------------------------
import test from 'node:test'
import assert from 'node:assert/strict'
import HttpError from '../utils/HttpError.js'
import asyncHandler from '../middleware/asyncHandler.js'
import errorHandler from '../middleware/errorHandler.js'

const fakeRes = () => ({
  code: null,
  body: null,
  status(c) { this.code = c; return this },
  json(b) { this.body = b; return this },
})

// Runs a handler the way express does and returns what the client would get
async function run(handler) {
  const res = fakeRes()
  let error
  await asyncHandler(handler)({}, res, (err) => { error = err })
  await new Promise((r) => setImmediate(r))
  if (error) errorHandler(error, {}, res, () => {})
  return res
}

test('an HttpError becomes its status code and message', async () => {
  const res = await run(async () => { throw new HttpError(409, 'This ticket was already taken') })
  assert.equal(res.code, 409)
  assert.deepEqual(res.body, { error: 'This ticket was already taken' })
})

test('an unexpected error becomes a generic 500 and leaks nothing', async () => {
  const original = console.error
  console.error = () => {}
  try {
    const res = await run(async () => { throw new Error('SELECT secret FROM ...') })
    assert.equal(res.code, 500)
    assert.deepEqual(res.body, { error: 'Server error' })
  } finally {
    console.error = original
  }
})

test('a successful handler answers normally and sends no error', async () => {
  const res = await run(async (req, r) => r.status(201).json({ id: 1 }))
  assert.equal(res.code, 201)
  assert.deepEqual(res.body, { id: 1 })
})

test('a synchronous throw inside the handler is caught too', async () => {
  const res = await run(() => { throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid status') })
  assert.equal(res.code, 400)
})

import test from 'node:test'
import assert from 'node:assert/strict'
import { planAssignments } from '../services/ticketAssignment.service.js'

const tech = (id, active = 0, total = 0, lastAssignedAt = null) => ({
  id, name: `Tech${id}`, active, total, lastAssignedAt,
})
const ticket = (id, extra = {}) => ({
  id, priority: 'medium', created_at: `2026-01-01T10:00:${String(id).padStart(2, '0')}Z`,
  assigned_to: null, ...extra,
})

test('a single free tech gets the ticket', () => {
  const plan = planAssignments([tech(1)], [ticket(10)])
  assert.deepEqual(plan.map((p) => [p.ticketId, p.techId, p.queued]), [[10, 1, false]])
})

test('the busy tech is skipped when another one is free', () => {
  const plan = planAssignments([tech(1, 1, 5), tech(2, 0, 9)], [ticket(10)])
  assert.equal(plan[0].techId, 2)
  assert.equal(plan[0].queued, false)
})

test('free techs share the work evenly (fewest handled first)', () => {
  const plan = planAssignments([tech(1, 0, 4), tech(2, 0, 2), tech(3, 0, 3)], [ticket(10)])
  assert.equal(plan[0].techId, 2)
})

test('equal workload: the one who waited longest goes first, then lowest id', () => {
  let plan = planAssignments([tech(1, 0, 2, 2000), tech(2, 0, 2, 1000)], [ticket(10)])
  assert.equal(plan[0].techId, 2)
  plan = planAssignments([tech(1), tech(2), tech(3)], [ticket(10)])
  assert.equal(plan[0].techId, 1)
})

test('several tickets at once are spread round-robin over free techs', () => {
  const plan = planAssignments([tech(1), tech(2), tech(3)], [ticket(1), ticket(2), ticket(3)])
  assert.deepEqual(plan.map((p) => p.techId).sort(), [1, 2, 3])
  assert.ok(plan.every((p) => !p.queued))
})

test('everybody busy: the ticket is queued on the FIRST tech', () => {
  const plan = planAssignments([tech(3, 1, 1), tech(1, 1, 7), tech(2, 2, 2)], [ticket(10)])
  assert.deepEqual(plan.map((p) => [p.techId, p.queued]), [[1, true]])
})

test('a queued ticket moves to another tech who finishes before the first one', () => {
  const queued = ticket(10, { assigned_to: 1 })
  const plan = planAssignments([tech(1, 1, 3), tech(2, 0, 3)], [queued])
  assert.equal(plan.length, 1)
  assert.equal(plan[0].techId, 2)
  assert.equal(plan[0].queued, false)
  assert.equal(plan[0].previousTechId, 1)
})

test('a queued ticket goes to the first tech when he is the one who finishes', () => {
  const plan = planAssignments([tech(1, 0, 3), tech(2, 1, 3)], [ticket(10, { assigned_to: 1 })])
  assert.equal(plan[0].techId, 1)
  assert.equal(plan[0].queued, false)
})

test('a ticket that is already queued stays untouched while nobody is free', () => {
  const plan = planAssignments([tech(1, 1), tech(2, 1)], [ticket(10, { assigned_to: 1 })])
  assert.deepEqual(plan, [])
})

test('after one tech becomes free only ONE queued ticket moves to him', () => {
  const pending = [ticket(1, { assigned_to: 1 }), ticket(2, { assigned_to: 1 })]
  const plan = planAssignments([tech(1, 1), tech(2, 0)], pending)
  assert.equal(plan.length, 1)
  assert.equal(plan[0].ticketId, 1) // the oldest waits the least
  assert.equal(plan[0].techId, 2)
})

test('high priority is served before older low priority tickets', () => {
  const pending = [ticket(1, { priority: 'low' }), ticket(2, { priority: 'high' })]
  const plan = planAssignments([tech(1)], pending)
  assert.equal(plan[0].ticketId, 2)
  assert.equal(plan[0].queued, false)
  assert.equal(plan[1].ticketId, 1)
  assert.equal(plan[1].queued, true)
})

test('no tech accounts at all: nothing is planned', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(planAssignments([], [ticket(1)]), [])
})

test('the input data is never modified', () => {
  const techs = [tech(1)]
  planAssignments(techs, [ticket(1)])
  assert.equal(techs[0].active, 0)
})

File: HttpError.js | Lines: 10

// An error that carries the HTTP status to answer with.
// Services throw it, the error middleware turns it into a JSON response.
export default class HttpError extends Error {
  constructor(status, message) {
    super(message)
    this.name = 'HttpError'
    this.status = status
  }
}
